==============================================================================
Amphitheatrum Sapientiae Aeternae (transcription)
Heinrich Khunrath (1560-1605) (1609)
Edition staged: Manuscript transcription with German translation (Internet Archive community upload). The printed edition appeared Hanau, 1609.
Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/heinrich-khunrath-amphitheatrum-mscpt.-u.-ueberset.
Rights: Public Domain. Author died 1605; work published 1609, well before 1930. No public-domain English translation exists.
Language: Latin with German translation
Staged for the Tower of Babel digital library (antarctic-labs.com).
Completeness/quality note: transcription of the text portion of Khunrath's theosophical-alchemical masterwork; OCR is noisy in places. The work's famous engraved plates (Oratory-Laboratory etc.) are visual and not representable in text.
==============================================================================
